Score S rapidly. Use FFT:
def fft_score(f):
  N = 1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()
  F=np.fft.rfft(f,N)
  c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:2*n-1]
  return 2*n*c.max(), c
Because f raw scaled; score independent but using raw bounded.

Let's structure code:

```
import numpy as np, time, math

def _fft_score(f):
    n=len(f); N=1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()
    F=np.fft.rfft(f,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:2*n-1]
    M=float(c.max())
    S=float(f.sum())
    if S <=0: return inf
    return 2.0*n*M/(S*S)
```
For f normalized S=1, same.

`_raw_from_norm(f, cap=1000.0)`: f positive; raw = f / max(f) * cap. But if f has negative due numerical (Basis? exp positive), clip. Ensure sum >0.01: if max raw*sum? Could be small if f very narrow: normalized f max ~1/(support length). If support < n/1000? raw min maybe 1000*minf/maxf; sum raw =1000*S/fmax. Since S=1, sum raw=1000/fmax. Lower bound fmax>=1/n, sum raw >=1000*n ≈6.9e6, ok.

def polish_sequence(f_norm, max_iters, deadline):
  f = positive normalized sum=1
  best_score=fast_score_norm(f); best_f=f.copy()
  lr=0.2
  for it...
    c,M,k=fft_conv_norm(f)
    score=2n*M
    if score<best...
    grad_f; valid... g=grad_f - grad_f.mean() # for constrained M
    scale=max(abs(g))
    if scale==0 break
    f_new=f*np.exp(-lr*g/scale)
    # small smoothing? maybe every 10: f_new = smooth(f_new, width=1)
    f_new/=sum
    nscore=fast_score_norm(f_new)
    if nscore<best_score: f=f_new; best...; lr=min(0.5,lr*1.02)
    else lr*=0.7
    # random: occasionally blend with best_f? maybe no.
  return best_f
```

Pseudo:
```
def beta_score(beta):
  phi = Q@beta
  if phi.max()>8: phi -= phi.max()
  f = np.exp(phi)
  f /= f.sum()
  c = fft_conv(f)
  M = c.max()
  return 2*n*M  # since sum1
```
For gradient, use c and M:
```
def beta_grad(beta, L=8):
  phi=...
  f=...
  c=...
  M=c.max()
  # top lags
  if L < 2*n-1:
    # get top L indices
    idx = np.argpartition(c, -L)[-L:]
  else idx all
  # weights
  t = max(1e-12, 0.1*M)
  w = np.exp((c[idx]-M)/t); w/=w.sum()
  gvec=np.zeros(n)
  for j,k in enumerate(idx): kk=int(k); ck=c[kk]
     lo=max(0,kk-n+1); hi=min(n-1,kk)
     if lo>hi: continue
     seg = f[lo:hi+1]
     # shifted f[k-j]: idx_shift = kk-lo down to kk-hi
     sh = f[kk-hi:kk-lo+1][::-1]
     gvec[lo:hi+1] += w[j]*(2*sh*seg - 2*ck*seg)
  # gvec sum near 0; subtract mean
  gvec -= gvec.mean()
  gb = Q.T @ gvec
  return gb
```
Check formula: shifted vector s_j=f[kk-j]. Valid range j lo..hi. The slice f[kk-hi:kk-lo+1] gives f[kk-hi],...,f[kk-lo]; reversed gives f[kk-lo] down to f[kk-hi]? For j=lo, kk-j=kk-lo; for j=hi, kk-j=kk-hi. So reversed of f[kk-hi:kk-lo+1] is [f[kk-lo],...,f[kk-hi]]. Good.
Term = 2*sh*seg - 2*ck*seg = 2*seg*(sh-ck). Since 2ck*seg scalar. Multiply weight. This is deltalog for log f? Yes.
For normalized f, gradient of L wrt log f = gvec. Good.
Then gb=Q.T@ gvec.

def small_search(m, n_restarts, iters, deadline):
  rng=...

       if not finite break
  return best
```

Let's outline final code:
```
import numpy as np, time, math, sys

_target=1.50274

def _fft_convNorm(f):
   n=len(f); N=1<<(2*n-2).bit_length()
   F=np.fft.rfft(f,N); c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:2*n-1]
   # clean negative tiny
   if c.size: c[ np.abs(c)<1e-15]=0?
   return c

def _raw(f):
   f=np.asarray(f,float)
   f=np.maximum(f,0)
   m=f.max()
   if m<1e-12: return [1.0]*len(f)
   raw=f/m*1000.0
   # if any >1000 due roundoff
   raw=np.minimum(raw,1000.0)
   return [float(x) for x in raw]
```

```
import numpy as np, time

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(10.0, float(budget_s)) - 5.0
    target = 1.50274
    best_score = float('inf')
    best_seq = None

    def raw_from_norm(f):
       f=np.asarray(f,dtype=np.float64)
       f=np.where(np.isfinite(f), f, 0.0)
       f=np.maximum(f,0.0)
       m=float(f.max())
       if m<=0.0 or not np.isfinite(m): return [1.0]
       raw=f*(1000.0/m)
       raw=np.minimum(raw,1000.0)
       if raw.sum() <0.01:
          raw=np.ones_like(raw)
       return [float(v) for v in raw]

    def consider(f, force_exact=False):
       nonlocal best_score,best_seq
       if f is None: return
       raw=raw_from_norm(f)
       # If forced or small length, exact eval. For large only if likely better surrogate.
       try:
          sc = float(evaluate_sequence(raw))
       except Exception:
          sc = float('inf')
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
          best_score=sc; best_seq=raw
```

    def fft_score_norm(f):
       n=len(f); S=f.sum(); if S<=0...; f=f/S
       if n<=300:
          c=np.convolve(f,f); M=c.max()
       else:
          N=1<<(2*n-2).bit_length(); F=np.fft.rfft(f,N); c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:2*n-1]; M=c.max()

```

    def small_search(m, restarts, iters, dl):
       local_best=inf; local_f=None
       for r in range(restarts):
          f=np.abs(rng.normal(size=m))+0.05; f/=f.sum()
          lr=0.3
          for it in range(iters):
             if time.monotonic()>dl: break
             c=np.convolve(f,f); M=c.max(); k=int(np.argmax(c)); sc=2.0*m*M
             if sc<local_best:
                local_best=sc; local_f=f.copy()
             if sc<best_score: consider(f) # exact maybe
             # gradient top1
             d = -2.0*M*f.copy()

             seg=f[lo:hi+1]; sh=f[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
             d[lo:hi+1] += 2.0*sh*seg
             d -= d.mean()
             scale=np.max(np.abs(d))
             if scale<=1e-300: break
             f *= np.exp(-lr*d/scale)
             f=np.maximum(f,1e-15); f/=f.sum()

             # adapt lr if improved local?

          consider(local_f)

Precompute N_large, Q:
```
    n = n_large
    N = 1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    K=14
    cols=[np.cos(np.pi*k*x), np.sin(np.pi*k*x) for k in 1..K]
    B=np.column_stack(cols)
    B -= B.mean(axis=0,keepdims=True)
    # QR; if n small? n large.
    Q,_=np.linalg.qr(B, mode='reduced')

    def phi_of(beta):
       phi=Q@beta
       phi -= phi.mean()
       return phi
    def beta_f(beta):
       phi=phi_of(beta)
       maxphi=phi.max()
       if maxphi > 6.0: phi -= maxphi
       f=np.exp(phi)
       f/=f.sum()
       return f
    def beta_score(beta):

```
seg = f[lo:hi+1]
sh = f[ki-hi:ki-lo+1][::-1]
glog[lo:hi+1] += ww*2*seg*(sh - c[ki])
```
Yes.
If top lags weighted, sum d zero? For each k, sum_j 2f_{k-j}f_j over valid =2c_k; sum_j -2c_k f_j = -2c_k; total zero. Good.
Gradient of score not including 2n constant; fine.
Line search uses exact max score. Smooth gradient may point to reduce top average, okay.

Direct polish gradient for top two equal weights: We use `d = -2.0*topc_sum * f` (sum of c_k). For each k add segment 2shseg. If weights equal and L=2, sum without 1/2; scale arbitrary. For L=1, d=-2Mf + segment. Good.
D zero mean because segment sum =2c_k; -2c_k sum f = -2c_k; per k zero. For L>1 sum zero. Good.

Beta range: `phi=Q@beta; phi -= phi.mean()`. Q features zero mean, mean ~0. If beta has components that create constant? subtract. If range >5 reject. In `beta_f`, if max phi > 8? We can do `phi = phi - phi.mean(); if phi.max()>6: phi -= phi.max()-6?` But scaling by subtracting max changes global scale only, score invariant. Use `if phi.max()-phi.min() > 5.0: phi = 5.0*(phi-np.mean? )?` But this changes shape (shrinks). For candidate line search we reject. For start projection, shrink beta to range ≤4.5.
In `beta_f`, we can safely do `phi = phi - phi.mean(); phi = np.clip(phi, -8,8)` to avoid overflow, accepting slight distortion for invalid. But gradient mismatch if clip. Since line search rejects range >5, current valid no clip. For safety, clip.

Parent fallback: If parent length large and values all zero? Use `np.ones(parent.size)`.

Small search considering exact: In `consider`, exact evaluator clips raw to 1000. If raw has many floats, list conversion maybe cost. For small many calls, okay. For large, use only top.
Maybe `consider` should optionally skip exact if length>1000 and not enough time. We'll define:
```
    def consider(f, exact=True):
       if not exact: return
       raw=...
       sc=evaluate_sequence(raw)
```
Call exact for small/large. For low-dim during loops, call exact only when `time.monotonic()-last_exact > 2.0` and sc surrogate < best_score -1e-5. Need `nonlocal last_exact`.
Simpler: `consider` exact always, but call rarely.

Implement `last_exact=[0.0]` list to avoid nonlocal? Use nonlocal okay.

Final code:

Let's proceed. Ensure indentation.

One concern: `np.linalg.qr(B, mode='reduced')` for B with more rows than cols may produce Q shape (n,P). If B has zero columns (if n=1?), not. n_large>=256.

In `beta_score`, uses `fft_score_large` which returns score,c. For line search many times, we only need score. Could write separate to avoid c? It returns c anyway; okay.

Memory in low-dim gradient: `glog` zeros each iter (56KB). Fine.

Small search uses `np.convolve` with f normalized; sum maybe changes after exp? We normalize after update. At start of each iter, f sum 1. Good.

Potential exact score for small sequence with raw scaled to 1000: If f has max 1/m, raw all ~1000m. If m=32, all up to 32000 >1000? Wait raw = f / max(f)*1000, max raw 1000. Sum raw =1000/max(f). For uniform m=32, max=1/32, sum=32000, fine. No clipping. If f varied, max raw1000.

If small sequence has f max very small? sum1 => max≥1/m, sum raw≥1000m. Good.

If shape direct Gaussian has f max maybe e.g. 10/n, raw max1000, many values tiny, sum raw=1000/fmax =1000 n/10=100n huge. Fine.

Let's include final exact validation of `best_seq` and if inf fallback. Return list.

Now final code.
</think>

```python
import numpy as np
import time

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, float(budget_s)) - 8.0
    best_exact = float("inf")
    best_seq = None
    last_exact = [0.0]

    def _raw_from_norm(f):
        f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
        if f.ndim != 1:
            f = f.ravel()
        f = np.where(np.isfinite(f), f, 0.0)
        f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
        m = float(f.max())
        if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 1e-300:
            f = np.ones(max(1, f.size), dtype=np.float64)
            m = f.max()
        raw = f * (1000.0 / m)
        raw = np.clip(raw, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if float(raw.sum()) < 0.01:
            raw = np.ones_like(raw)
        return [float(x) for x in raw]

    def _consider(f):
        nonlocal best_exact, best_seq
        if t0 + 3.0 > deadline or f is None:
            return
        if time.monotonic() - last_exact[0] < 0.5:
            return
        try:
            raw = _raw_from_norm(f)
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(raw))
        except Exception:
            sc = float("inf")
        last_exact[0] = time.monotonic()
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
            best_exact = sc
            best_seq = raw

    def _fft_score_c(f, N=None, n=None):
        n = len(f) if n is None else n
        if N is None:
            N = 1 << (2 * n - 2).bit_length()
        F = np.fft.rfft(f, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, N)[:2 * n - 1]
        c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
        return 2.0 * n * float(c.max()), c

    # Small-length search: if it finds a strong candidate, it remains a valid fallback.
    def _small_search(m, restarts, iters):
        if m <= 1:
            return
        local_best = float("inf")
        local_f = None
        for _ in range(restarts):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120.0:
                break
            f = np.abs(rng.normal(size=m)) + 0.05
            f /= f.sum()
            lr = 0.25
            for _ in range(iters):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120.0:
                    break
                try:
                    c = np.convolve(f, f)
                    M = float(c.max())
                    k = int(np.argmax(c))
                    sc = 2.0 * m * M
                except Exception:
                    break
                if sc < local_best:
                    local_best = sc
                    local_f = f.copy()
                d = -2.0 * M * f.copy()
                lo = max(0, k - m + 1)
                hi = min(m - 1, k)
                seg = f[lo:hi + 1]
                sh = f[k - hi:k - lo + 1][::-1]
                d[lo:hi + 1] += 2.0 * sh * seg
                d -= d.mean()
                scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
                if scale <= 1e-300:
                    break
                improved = False
                for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.2):
                    trial = f * np.exp(-lr * mult * d / scale)
                    trial = np.maximum(trial, 1e-15)
                    trial /= trial.sum()
                    tsc = 2.0 * m * float(np.convolve(trial, trial).max())
                    if tsc < sc - 1e-12:
                        f = trial
                        sc = tsc
                        improved = True
                        break
                lr = min(0.6, lr * 1.05) if improved else max(0.03, lr * 0.75)

        if local_f is not None:
            _consider(local_f)

    _small_search(3, 10, 40)
    _small_search(4, 18, 80)
    _small_search(5, 18, 80)
    _small_search(6, 18, 80)
    _small_search(8, 16, 80)
    _small_search(10, 14, 70)
    _small_search(12, 12, 60)
    _small_search(16, 10, 60)
    _small_search(24, 8, 50)
    _small_search(32, 8, 45)

    # Choose large-work resolution from the supplied parent when reasonable.
    parent = None
    try:
        par = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        par = np.where(np.isfinite(par), par, 0.0)
        par = np.clip(par, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if par.ndim == 1 and par.size >= 128 and float(par.sum()) > 0.01:
            parent = par / par.sum()
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    n = int(parent.size) if parent is not None else 4096
    N = 1 << (2 * n - 2).bit_length()
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(n)

    def _large_score(f):
        return _fft_score_c(f, N, n)[0]

    # Low-dimensional orthonormal cosine/sine feature matrix.
    K = 14
    cols = []
    for k in range(1, K + 1):
        a = np.pi * k * x
        cols.append(np.cos(a))
        cols.append(np.sin(a))
    B = np.column_stack(cols)
    B -= B.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    try:
        Q, _ = np.linalg.qr(B, mode="reduced")
    except Exception:
        Q, _ = np.linalg.svd(B, full_matrices=False)
    P = Q.shape[1]

    def _phi(beta):
        phi = Q @ beta
        phi -= float(phi.mean())
        return phi

    def _beta_f(beta):
        phi = _phi(beta)
        phi = np.clip(phi, -8.0, 8.0)
        phi = phi - float(phi.max())
        f = np.exp(phi)
        f = np.maximum(f, 1e-300)
        f /= f.sum()
        return f

    def _beta_score(beta):
        return _large_score(_beta_f(beta))

    def _beta_range(beta):
        phi = Q @ beta
        return float(phi.max() - phi.min())

    def _add_beta(starts, beta):
        if beta is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(beta)):
            return
        beta = beta.astype(np.float64).copy()
        r = _beta_range(beta)
        if r > 4.5:
            beta *= 4.5 / max(1e-12, r)
        beta = np.clip(beta, -5.0, 5.0)
        if r > 0.05:
            starts.append(beta)

    def _add_log(starts, logv):
        lv = np.asarray(logv, dtype=np.float64)
        lv = np.where(np.isfinite(lv), lv, 0.0)
        lv -= float(lv.mean())
        if lv.size != n:
            return
        c = Q.T @ lv
        _add_beta(starts, c)

    starts = []

    # Parent projected into the low-dimensional smooth subspace.
    if parent is not None and parent.size == n:
        _add_log(starts, np.log(np.maximum(parent, 1e-15)))

    # Deterministic shape priors: off-center Gaussians, exponentials, cosine dips, tilts.
    for mu in np.arange(0.08, 0.925, 0.05):
        for sig in (0.18, 0.28, 0.40, 0.55, 0.75, 1.05):
            _add_log(starts, -0.5 * ((x - mu) / sig) ** 2)
    for al in (0.3, 0.7, 1.2, 2.0, 3.2):
        _add_log(starts, -al * x)
        _add_log(starts, al * (x - 1.0))
    for de in (0.2, 0.45, 0.7, 0.95):
        ph = 1.0 - de * np.cos(np.pi * x)
        _add_log(starts, np.log(np.maximum(ph, 1e-8)))
    for a in (-2.0, -1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        _add_log(starts, a * (x - 0.5))

    # A few random smooth starts.
    for _ in range(18):
        _add_beta(starts, rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, P))
    for _ in range(18):
        lv = Q @ rng.normal(0.0, 0.8, P)
        ph = 1.0 + 0.45 * lv
        _add_log(starts, np.log(np.maximum(ph, 1e-6)))

    # Score all starts, keep the best low-dimensional seeds.
    scored = []
    for b in starts:
        try:
            scored.append((_beta_score(b), b))
        except Exception:
            pass
    scored.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
    top_betas = [b for _, b in scored[:12]]

    def _optimize_beta(beta, max_it, dl):
        if max_it <= 0:
            return beta, float("inf")
        try:
            beta = np.asarray(beta, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            sc, c = _fft_score_c(_beta_f(beta), N, n)
        except Exception:
            return beta, float("inf")
        best_beta = beta.copy()
        best_sc = sc
        lr = 0.6
        L = 6
        for it in range(max_it):
            if time.monotonic() > dl - 20.0:
                break
            f = _beta_f(beta)
            sc, c = _fft_score_c(f, N, n)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_beta = beta.copy()
                if sc < best_exact or it % 300 == 0:
                    _consider(f)

            M = float(c.max())
            if M <= 0.0:
                break
            if c.size > L:
                top = np.argpartition(c, -L)[-L:]
            else:
                top = np.arange(c.size)
            ct = c[top]
            t = max(1e-300, 0.16 * float(ct.max()))
            w = np.exp((ct - ct.max()) / t)
            w /= w.sum()

            glog = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            for kk, ck, ww in zip(top, ct, w):
                ki = int(kk)
                lo = max(0, ki - n + 1)
                hi = min(n - 1, ki)
                if lo > hi:
                    continue
                seg = f[lo:hi + 1]
                sh = f[ki - hi:ki - lo + 1][::-1]
                glog[lo:hi + 1] += ww * 2.0 * seg * (sh - ck)
            glog -= glog.mean()
            gb = Q.T @ glog
            gbn = float(np.max(np.abs(gb)))
            improved = False
            if gbn > 1e-30:
                for mult in (1.0, 0.45, 0.18):
                    cand = beta - lr * mult * gb / gbn
                    cand = np.clip(cand, -5.0, 5.0)
                    if _beta_range(cand) > 5.2:
                        continue
                    try:
                        nsc = _beta_score(cand)
                    except Exception:
                        nsc = float("inf")
                    if np.isfinite(nsc) and nsc < sc - 1e-12:
                        beta = cand
                        sc = nsc
                        improved = True
                        break
            if improved:
                lr = min(1.2, lr * 1.06)
            else:
                lr *= 0.72
                if lr < 0.07:
                    cand = beta + rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, P)
                    cand = np.clip(cand, -5.0, 5.0)
                    if _beta_range(cand) <= 5.2:
                        nsc = _beta_score(cand)
                        if np.isfinite(nsc) and nsc < best_sc * 1.08:
                            beta = cand
                            sc = nsc
                            lr = 0.35
        return best_beta, best_sc

    # Deepen the best seeds.
    if top_betas:
        for bi, b0 in enumerate(top_betas):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120.0:
                break
            left = deadline - 120.0 - time.monotonic()
            if left <= 30:
                break
            max_it = int(min(2200, max(300, left / max(1, len(top_betas) - bi))))
            beta, sc = _optimize_beta(b0, max_it, deadline - 20.0)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                _consider(_beta_f(beta))

    # Save the best low-dimensional large-n candidate for polishing.
    large_best_norm = None
    if top_betas:
        # Re-evaluate top handful quickly and keep best.
        best_lb = float("inf")
        best_bf = None
        for b0 in top_betas[:8]:
            try:
                f = _beta_f(b0)
                sc = _large_score(f)
                if sc < best_lb:
                    best_lb = sc
                    best_bf = f
            except Exception:
                pass
        if best_bf is not None:
            large_best_norm = best_bf
            _consider(large_best_norm)

    # Full-length projected subgradient polish on the best large candidate.
    def _polish_large(f, dl):
        f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        f = np.where(np.isfinite(f), f, 0.0)
        f = np.maximum(f, 1e-15)
        f /= f.sum()
        best_f = f.copy()
        best_sc, _ = _fft_score_c(f, N, n)
        lr = 0.18
        use_top = 2
        for _ in range(50000):
            if time.monotonic() > dl - 20.0:
                break
            sc, c = _fft_score_c(f, N, n)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_f = f.copy()
                if sc < best_exact or (time.monotonic() - last_exact[0] > 2.0):
                    _consider(f)
            M = float(c.max())
            if M <= 0.0:
                break
            if c.size > use_top:
                top = np.argpartition(c, -use_top)[-use_top:]
            else:
                top = np.arange(c.size)
            d = -2.0 * float(np.sum(c[top])) * f
            for kk in top:
                ki = int(kk)
                lo = max(0, ki - n + 1)
                hi = min(n - 1, ki)
                if lo > hi:
                    continue
                seg = f[lo:hi + 1]
                sh = f[ki - hi:ki - lo + 1][::-1]
                d[lo:hi + 1] += 2.0 * sh * seg
            d -= d.mean()
            scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
            if scale <= 1e-300:
                break
            improved = False
            for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.22):
                trial = f * np.exp(-lr * mult * d / scale)
                trial = np.maximum(trial, 1e-15)
                trial /= trial.sum()
                tsc = _fft_score_c(trial, N, n)[0]
                if np.isfinite(tsc) and tsc < sc - 1e-12:
                    f = trial
                    sc = tsc
                    improved = True
                    break
            if improved:
                lr = min(0.6, lr * 1.04)
            else:
                lr *= 0.75
                if lr < 0.025:
                    f = 0.65 * f + 0.35 * best_f
                    f = np.maximum(f, 1e-15)
                    f /= f.sum()
                    lr = 0.12
        return best_f, best_sc

    if large_best_norm is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 80.0:
        pf, psc = _polish_large(large_best_norm, deadline - 15.0)
        _consider(pf)

    # Final safety: evaluate the parent construction and a simple constant.
    if parent is not None:
        _consider(parent)
    try:
        _consider(np.ones(max(2, min(n, 256)), dtype=np.float64))
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_seq is None or not best_seq or not all(np.isfinite(x) for x in best_seq):
        return [1.0, 1.0]

    # Force final exact validation before returning.
    try:
        final_sc = float(evaluate_sequence(best_seq))
        if not np.isfinite(final_sc):
            return [1.0, 1.0]
    except Exception:
        pass

    return best_seq
```
